# P01/I03/S01/C15 experiment disposition ledger

- baseline: `92cdd9114046115d45451153c6ebad3b425db36e`
- experiment X1: 9488f2000f0a31485679c39a568ff2a6d9d6f28f; first parent fa9c738577110c893b7b156c0e68b68fdeec6e51.
- experiment X2: 4225fae1466aef093f5f22f69bd8c17c6e4420d7; first parent 93fbc8e59f4b58a26520915ac1285d3fe8f54622.
- Extraction used both exact function-context and U0 diffs plus both exact diff-tree manifests.
- Each U0 side maps to its smallest enclosing fully qualified item and every declaration beginning in that side. This refines the initial file-global 498-name result to 511 fully qualified occurrences; 498 is audit history, not accepted coverage.
- Every surviving direct caller of an experiment-introduced descendant is recorded, including introduced helper-to-helper and adjacent governing callers.
- Relationship extraction is receiver/type/module qualified. Method calls use the concrete receiver type from compiler THIR; associated and free calls use the resolved local definition path. A shared simple name is never evidence.
- The re-extraction removed 757 simple-name collision rows (`finish`: 81, `try_new`: 500, `from`: 174, `run`: 2) and retained stable IDs for the 400 compiler-resolved relationships.
- Decisions use ownership, cohesion, order, lifetime, failure, invariant, and test intent. Size signals are not a disposition rationale.

## Rationale codes

- R-GOVERNING-FLOW: existing orchestration retains order, cleanup, and diagnostics while delegating.
- R-OPERATION-PHASE: one validation, lowering, encoding, acquisition, publication, or failure phase.
- R-MODEL: one coherent phase, invariant, or ownership state.
- R-TEST-INTENT: one focused fixture, oracle, assertion, or source-contract check.
- R-OWNERSHIP: the import or impl remains with its governing module or type.
- R-RELATIONSHIP: the call preserves the current phase, order, lifetime, or test-intent handoff.
- R-MECHANICAL-OWNERSHIP: tuple-return or lexical-parser fragmentation moved coupled state or logic away from its owner.
- R-RELATIONSHIP-REMEDIATION: the call touches that same open ownership repair.
- R-SUPERSEDED-DUPLICATE: X2 c10_future_backdrop_scene is absent; X1 c10_future_backdrop_scene_for_test survives with that purpose.
- R-SUPERSEDED-ABSENCE: later behavior, test, or import evolution removed the historical item and no helper relationship survives.

## T02 lifecycle and resource closure

- Domain: production portions of `src/backend.rs`, `src/frame.rs`, `src/gpu_transaction.rs`, `src/renderer.rs`, and `src/resource.rs`.
- Historical evidence: both exact first-parent experiment pairs were inspected with function-context and zero-context Rust diffs plus their diff-tree manifests: `fa9c738577110c893b7b156c0e68b68fdeec6e51..9488f2000f0a31485679c39a568ff2a6d9d6f28f` and `93fbc8e59f4b58a26520915ac1285d3fe8f54622..4225fae1466aef093f5f22f69bd8c17c6e4420d7`.
- Current-source evidence: all 136 T02-domain ITEM occurrences and 92 surviving REL relationships were inspected at immutable baseline `92cdd9114046115d45451153c6ebad3b425db36e`.
- Accepted T01 schema evidence: 511 ITEM rows, 400 REL rows, and 757 rejected collision rows; no accepted remediation row is unowned or multiply owned.
- Cohesion result: backend device/surface acquisition and presentation failure paths, frame declaration/scheduling/lowering validation phases, graph transaction submission and headless/presented completion, renderer identity/preparation/execution/publication/resume dispatch, and resource kernel validation/serialization remain genuine governing flows, operation/failure boundaries, models, ownership boundaries, or focused test-intent helpers. Their bundles retain phase-owned state; their relationships preserve ordering, cleanup, publication, or lifetime handoff. No forwarding, bundle, ordering, cleanup, publication, or lifetime regression was found.
- Open or assigned T02 remediations: **0**.

## T03 graph raster and model closure

- Domain: the one accepted T03 remediation in the production portion of `src/pass.rs`; the other graph, pass, shader, capability, glyph, raster, recording, and scene dispositions remain unchanged.
- Corrected remediation: **1** (`ITEM-X2-PA-069`).
- Corrected descendant/source evidence: the historical positional `pass::PreparedGraphResourceBindings` alias is superseded in this task's committed source by private `pass::AcquiredGraphBindings` at `src/pass.rs`, whose `runtime_bindings` and `gaussian_kernel_bindings` fields name the independently owned runtime-resource and Gaussian-kernel binding maps.
- Preserved acquisition evidence: `pass::acquire_prepared_graph_resources` and retained relationship `REL-0276` remain the single acquisition phase and consumer handoff; the consumer moves the two named fields into the existing `PreparedGraph` fields.
- Preservation result: resource acquisition remains before kernel acquisition; duplicate diagnostics, frame-scope lease ownership, error/drop cleanup, color-filter binding creation, pass realization, and final `PreparedGraph` installation are unchanged.
- Open or assigned T03 remediations: **0**.

## T04 test-code cohesion closure

- Domain: the `StaticSourceScanForTest` lexical scanner, its four parser helpers, and their four governing relationships in `src/tests.rs`.
- Corrected remediation: **10 ITEM occurrences and 4 REL relationships**.
- Corrected descendant/source evidence: `StaticSourceScanForTest` privately owns `mask_non_code`, `raw_string_end`, `cooked_string_end`, and `char_literal_end`; `new` calls each with `Self::`, and every row retains its immutable historical anchor plus a semantic T04 committed-descendant anchor without mutable-checkout line coordinates.
- Preservation evidence: `source_scanner_ignores_non_code_routes_and_braces_and_rejects_duplicate_markers` characterizes raw strings, cooked strings, character and byte-character literals, line and nested block comments, non-code masking, brace scanning, and duplicate executable-marker rejection.
- Schema evidence: all 511 ITEM rows, 400 REL rows, and 757 rejected collision rows remain present; stable IDs, experiment/parent identities, historical hunk anchors, duplicate links, relationship reverse links, and relationship endpoints remain intact.
- Open or assigned T04 remediations: **0**.
